# The messages the wire schemas decode in the offline suite (#2290),
# written by tools/schema_corpus.py; tests/test_schema_round_trip.py
# re-encodes each. One per line: schema, codec, field version, what the
# decoder was given (JSON), the bytes it read (hex), and the test it
# came from. Regenerate after a schema or a capture changes. A message
# the Mirror builds as the test runs carries its time (an OCI describe
# head's), so that line changes on each regeneration.

#   96 random or cut-short bytes, not messages
